**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- auto-bandwidth enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](MPLS%20TE命令.files/image001.png){#图片 26 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x21423380}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_28603434}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[auto-bandwidth enable]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1347068284}[命令用来全局开启自动带宽调整功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[并配置出口速率采样的时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo auto-bandwidth enable]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_103213222}[命令用来全局关闭自动带宽调整功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2011169232}

[**[auto-bandwidth]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x453720882}[ **enable** \[ **sample-interval** *seconds* \]]{lang="FR"}

[**[undo auto-bandwidth enable]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x573377868}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_336413351}

[[全局自动带宽调整功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1481025789}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_541965937}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1662835419}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x437492760}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x564147249}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_196038905}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1286349743}

[**[sample-interval]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x70826156}[ *seconds*]{lang="FR"}[：指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[为配置了自动带宽调整功能的隧道进行出口速率采样的时间间隔]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[seconds]{lang="FR"}*[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="FR"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[604800]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[单位为秒]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[缺省值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[300]{lang="FR"}[秒。建议使用缺省值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_336347815}

[[自动带宽调整功能是指对隧道的平均出口速率进行测量，并周期性地根据测量结果调整隧道的带宽，以便根据用户的业务量自动调整为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_905946873}[隧道分配的带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[出口速率采样功能是指设备根据在出口速率采样时间间隔内通过的流量大小，计算平均出口速率。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x493843633}

[[通过本命令全局开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1579250}[自动带宽调整功能后，还需要在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图下执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth adjustment]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，才能实现对该隧道进行自动带宽调整。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_613010634}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1056771587}[全局开启自动带宽调整功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[配置对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的出口速率每隔]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[分钟（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[600]{lang="EN-US"}[秒）进行一次采样。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1356312589}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-te\] auto-bandwidth enable sample-interval 600]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1715651594}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2146975611}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[reset mpls te auto-bandwidth-adjustment timers]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1023006778}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- auto-tunnel backup**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x492991665}[命令用来全局开启自动隧道备份功能，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[自动隧道备份视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_164803049}[命令用来全局关闭自动隧道备份功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x898219058}

[**[auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2131364875}

[**[undo auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1486435720}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1824473609}

[[自动隧道备份功能处于全局关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_507090053}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x492926129}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1797320770}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_635102056}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1809516426}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1755181957}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1790006740}

[[通过本命令全局开启自动隧道备份功能，并通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1187435113}**[tunnel-number]{lang="FR"}**[命令配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[自动创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的接口编号范围后，本地设备会自动为经过本设备、且开启了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[功能的所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道建立两条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道：一条链路保护的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道和一条节点保护的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1804975604}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x493515952}[命令后，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[会]{style="font-family:宋体"}[删除所有已经自动建立的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[倒数第二跳节点作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x194149412}[PLR]{lang="EN-US"}[时，只自动创建链路保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，不会自动创建节点保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1309281328}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2043254981}[全局]{style="font-family:宋体"}[开启自动隧道备份功能，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[自动隧道备份视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1967561553}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te\] auto-tunnel backup]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te-auto-bk\]]{lang="FR"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- destination**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[destination]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x834462469}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的隧道目的端地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo destination]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x871773233}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:
黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2651968}

[**[destination]{lang="EN-US"}**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x317212222}*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[undo destination]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_441706691}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1879910055}

[[未指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2081500271}[接口的隧道目的端地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1620926266}

[[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1497778568}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1156821882}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_828616566}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_273471527}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x773406342}

[*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1092861476}[：隧道的目的端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_73851663}

[[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2121814054}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L2VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPLS]{lang="EN-US"}[根据本命令配置的隧道目的端地址，选择承载]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[业务的公网隧道。即：远端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[的地址与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的隧道目的端地址相同时，该捆绑隧道可以作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L2VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPLS]{lang="EN-US"}[的公网隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[建议为成员接口和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1620991802}[接口配置相同的目的端地址。如果不同，则需要确保通过成员接口能够到达]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的目的端地址；否则，会导致流量转发不通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x73816237}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_47631170}[设置隧道捆绑接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle2]{lang="EN-US"}[的隧道目的端地址为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2.2.2.2]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1159820200}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel-bundle 2]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-tunnel-bundle2\] destination 2.2.2.2]{lang="EN-US"}

:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- display isis mpls te advertisement**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display isis mpls te advertisement]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_164737513}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS TEDB]{lang="FR"}[中的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1874336220}

[**[display isis mpls te]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x346987747}[ **advertisement** \[ \[ **level-1** \| **level-2** \] \| \[ **originate-system** *system-id* \| **local** \] \| **verbose** \] \* \[ *process-id* \]]{lang="FR"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2050017960}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x921180071}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_259260624}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_332281255}

[[network-operator]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x492926128}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1797255234}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1694121914}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1504568305}

[**[level-1]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1820182558}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="FR"}[路由器的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[level-2]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1873732356}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="FR"}[路由器的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[originate-system]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2054194809}[ *system-id*]{lang="FR"}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[显示指定系统发布的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[system-id]{lang="FR"}*[为系统]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ID]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[形式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[XXXX.XXXX.XXXX]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[local]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_967665750}[：显示本地发布的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x137782925}[：显示详细信息。如果不指定本参数，则显示简要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2104701970}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x372660427}

[[执行本命令时]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x933363009}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[如果没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[level-1]{lang="FR"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[level-2]{lang="FR"}**[参数，则同时显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="FR"}[路由器和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="FR"}[路由器的信息；如果没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[originate-system]{lang="FR"}**[ *system-id*]{lang="FR"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[local]{lang="FR"}**[参数，则显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[系统]{style="font-family:宋体"}[发布的信息]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_243816141}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x493515955}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS TEDB]{lang="FR"}[中]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="FR"}[路由器的链路和节点简要信息]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[level-1]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1633963526}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="EN-US"}[路由器的网络信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[level-2]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2027190128}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[路由器的网络信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[local]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x493515957}[：显示本地发布的网络信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[lsp-id]{lang="EN-US"}***[ lsp-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x193821732}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的网络信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[lsp-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[标识，形式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SYSID*.*Pseudonode ID-fragment num]{lang="EN-US"}[，其中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SYSID]{lang="EN-US"}[是产生该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-GB"}[的节点或伪节点的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SystemID]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Pseudonode ID]{lang="EN-US"}[是伪节点]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[fragment num]{lang="EN-US"}[是该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的分片号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x526011889}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_673936646}

[[执行本命令时]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_80022978}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[如果没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[level-1]{lang="FR"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[level-2]{lang="FR"}**[参数，则同时显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="FR"}[路由器和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="FR"}[路由器的信息；如果没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[local]{lang="FR"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[lsp-id]{lang="FR"}***[ lsp-id]{lang="FR"}*[参数，则显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[所有网络的信息]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[isis]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1072436916}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[级别的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[level-1]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_175829028}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[level-1]{lang="EN-US"}[级别的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[level-2]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_586949962}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[级别的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ospf area]{lang="EN-US"}***[ area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1072502452}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[区域的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[区域的标识，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[link]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_577314199}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址对应链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*[为接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[network]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1988382897}[：显示所有广播和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[NBMA]{lang="EN-US"}[网络的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[node]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1072305844}[：显示节点相关的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。如果没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[local]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mpls-lsr-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[参数，则显示所有节点相关的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[local]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1753050377}[：显示本地节点相关的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[mpls-lsr-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x526376165}[：显示指定节点相关的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mpls-lsr-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为节点的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[summary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1072371380}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[的摘要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- display ospf mpls te advertisement**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ospf mpls te advertisement]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x92547537}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[中的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x778919028}

[**[display ospf]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ *process-id* \] \[ **area** *area-id* \] **mpls te advertisement** \[ **originate-router** *advertising-router-id* \| **self-originate** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1878976882}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1621188416}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x904144745}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1020642119}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1629479725}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x189801127}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_79870475}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x304364352}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1621253952}

[*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x441806345}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果未指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[area]{lang="EN-US"}***[ area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1761651156}[：显示指定区域的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为区域的标识，可以是十进制整数（取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，系统会将其转换成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址格式）或者是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址格式。如果未指定本参数，则显示所有区域的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[originate-router ]{lang="EN-US"}***[advertising-router-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1045999196}[：显示指定路由器发布的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[advertising-router-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[路由器的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Router ID]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[self-originate]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_936467499}[：显示本地路由器自己产生的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1924547477}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1621581632}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[中的链路和节点信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- display ospf mpls te network**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ospf mpls te network]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1792414636}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF TEDB]{lang="EN-US"}[中的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Network]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_584592023}

[**[display ospf]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ *process-id* \] \[ **area** *area-id* \] **mpls te network** \[ **originate-router** *advertising-router-id* \| **self-originate** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1107694943}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_527202962}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_865274514}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1618081675}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2126876816}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1357528354}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x940652221}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1107629407}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1813568836}

[*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x328371914}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果未指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[area]{lang="EN-US"}***[ area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1773701741}[：显示指定区域的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示区域的标识，可以是十进制整数（取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，系统会将其转换成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址格式）或者是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址格式*。*如果未指定本参数，则显示所有区域的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[originate-router ]{lang="EN-US"}***[advertising-router-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1514482954}[：显示指定路由器发布的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[advertising-router-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[路由器的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Router ID]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[self-originate]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x751595259}[：显示本地路由器自己产生的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1107301727}

[*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1862044091}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[process-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果未指定本参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[进程的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[area]{lang="EN-US"}***[ area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1689789999}[：显示指定区域的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[area-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示区域的标识，可以是十进制整数（取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，系统会将其转换成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址格式）或者是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址格式。如果未指定本参数，则显示所有区域的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- ds-te bc-model**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[ds-te bc-model]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x201013050}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF DS-TE]{lang="FR"}[模式下使用的带宽约束模型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ds-te bc-model]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1659861982}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1574260875}

[**[ds-te bc-model mam]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1909965396}

[**[undo]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_724920787}[ **ds-te bc-model**]{lang="FR"}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1699275532}

[[IETF DS-TE]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1902497293}[模式的带宽约束模型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RDM]{lang="FR"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Russian Dolls Model]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[俄罗斯套娃模型]{style="font-family:宋体"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:
黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_6455614}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1835483645}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1889634606}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1954854500}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_438630469}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1634088131}

[**[mam]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1040205323}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[指定带宽约束模型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAM]{lang="FR"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Maximum Allocation Model]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[最大分配模型]{style="font-family:宋体"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1902431757}

[[RDM]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_404658172}[模型可以限制多种服务类型流量（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[）的共用带宽，允许多种]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[间共享使用带宽，而不是限制某一种]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[的带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RDM]{lang="EN-US"}[与建立优先级]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[保持优先级配合，可以实现]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[间的带宽隔离。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RDM]{lang="EN-US"}[比较适用于属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[的流量不平稳、可能存在突发流量的情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[MAM]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1592370067}[模型可以限制某一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[在接口上占用的带宽总和，即隔离]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[之间的带宽使用。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAM]{lang="EN-US"}[不需要与建立优先级]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[保持优先级配合，就可以实现]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[间的带宽隔离。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAM]{lang="EN-US"}[的特点是比较直观，配置较为容易。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAM]{lang="EN-US"}[比较适用于属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[的流量较为平稳、不存在突发流量的情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1203021615}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[本命令仅对]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1927210315}[模式有效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Prestandard DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下，只能使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RDM]{lang="EN-US"}[带宽约束模型。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x852185030}[IETF DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下修改带宽约束模型后，设备上所有预留带宽非零的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[都将被拆除，并重新建立。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_214870782}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_848228401}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下带宽约束模型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAM]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1902890509}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te\] ds-te bc-model mam]{lang="FR"}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1147538093}

宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 3]{lang="EN-US"}[）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[priority ]{lang="EN-US"}***[priority-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x724893447}[：指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[priority-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[优先级，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[7]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[如果流量属于某个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1452293429}[，则传输该流量的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的建立优先级或保持优先级必须是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="EN-US"}[中该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x845316789}**[ds-te te-class]{lang="FR"}**[命令配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[时]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[输入的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[CT]{lang="FR"}[和优先级不要与已有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="FR"}[和优先级相同。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_326784339}**[undo ds-te te-class]{lang="FR"}**[命令恢复缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[时]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[恢复的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[CT]{lang="FR"}[和优先级不要与已有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="FR"}[和优先级相同。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[修改任意的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1211082666}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[后]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[设备都会通知]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:

宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[接口的带宽信息]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- ds-te mode**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[ds-te mode]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x480226104}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="FR"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ds-te mode]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_997368455}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2021074132}

[**[ietf]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1216636460}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="FR"}[模式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="FR"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1684859968}

[[Prestandard]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1009206834}[模式和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="FR"}[模式具有如下区别]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[请根据服务类型的数量、所需带宽约束模型等选择合适的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="FR"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Prestandard]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_37424479}[模式支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="FR"}[个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="FR"}[（]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="FR"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="FR"}[），]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="FR"}[种优先级]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[最大支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[16]{lang="FR"}[个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="FR"}[模式支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="FR"}[个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="FR"}[（]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="FR"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="FR"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 2]{lang="FR"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 3]{lang="FR"}[），]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="FR"}[种优先级]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[最大支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="FR"}[个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="FR"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Prestandard]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1214583596}[模式下不可以通过配置改变]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下可以通过配置改变]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TE class]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Prestandard]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1476513406}[模式只支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RDM]{lang="FR"}[模型]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="FR"}[模式支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RDM]{lang="FR"}[模型和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MAM]{lang="FR"}[模型。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Prestandard]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826058379}[模式为自定义模式，无法与所有厂商设备互通；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式为根据]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RFC]{lang="EN-US"}[标准实现的模式，可以与其他厂商设备互通。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x748444343}[修改]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="FR"}[模式后]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[设备上所有的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="FR"}[都将被拆除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[并重新建立。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- fast-reroute timer**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[fast-reroute timer]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825796235}[命令用来配置在多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道中进行优选的时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo fast-reroute timer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_230234184}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_574851933}

[**[fast-reroute timer ]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_394480810}*[interval]{lang="FR"}*

[**[undo fast-reroute timer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1885806908}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1715828813}

[[在多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_262580469}[隧道中进行优选的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[300]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1118153855}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1685537852}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826254986}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1047187902}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x261896243}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1194795674}

[*[interval]{lang="FR"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1000787099}[：在多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道中进行优选的时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[604800]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[表示不会定期进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道优选。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_358794260}

[[如果为一条主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_796558514}[指定了多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[会从中选择一条最优的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，当主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[出现故障时，将流量切换到该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道转发。在某些情况下（如]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的可预留带宽发生变化），当前的最优隧道可能不是之前选中的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。因此，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[需要周期性地选择最优的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。通过本命令可以调整]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道优选的周期。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，如果流量已经从主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1345879890}[切换到了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，则不再进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道优选。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x634207076}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826320522}[配置在多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道中进行优选的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[120]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x724827911}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-te\] fast-reroute timer 120]{lang="NO-BOK"}
:::

[]{#_Toc404790944}[]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1107629403}[]{#_Toc349810087}[]{#_Toc347911122}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- interface tunnel-bundle**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[interface tunnel-bundle]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_512029992}[命令用来创建负载分担模式的隧道捆绑接口（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口），并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1460716401}**[interface tunnel-bundle]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令用来删除指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_800516562}

[**[interface tunnel-bundle]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *number*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1630436197}

[**[undo ]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1107301723}**[interface tunnel-bundle]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *number*]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1130115765}

[[设备上不存在任何]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_692375031}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2075989331}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_467275207}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1107236187}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2080494916}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1601130647}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x569236530}

[*[number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458257925}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的编号。不同型号的设备支持的取值范围不同，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_248984153}

[[在负载分担模式的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x755179897}[接口下重复执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[member interface]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，可以为该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口指定多个成员接口------]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道接口，形成一个捆绑隧道，使得流量的出接口为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口时，该流量能够在多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道间进行负载分担。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1250959929}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1522314203}[创建负载分担模式的隧道捆绑接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle2]{lang="EN-US"}[，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname-tunnel-bundle2\]]{lang="EN-US"}

{#_Toc342571846}[]{#_Toc340481283}[]{#_Toc342571847}[]{#_Toc340481284}[]{#_Toc342571848}[]{#_Toc340481285}[]{#_Toc342571849}[]{#_Toc340481286}[]{#_Toc342571850}[]{#_Toc340481287}[]{#_Toc342571851}[]{#_Toc340481288}[]{#_Toc342571852}[]{#_Toc340481289}[]{#_Toc342571853}[]{#_Toc340481290}[]{#_Toc342571854}[]{#_Toc340481291}[]{#_Toc342571855}[]{#_Toc340481292}[]{#_Toc342571856}[]{#_Toc340481293}[]{#_Toc342571857}[]{#_Toc340481294}[]{#_Toc342571858}[]{#_Toc340481295}[]{#_Toc342571859}[]{#_Toc340481296}[]{#_Toc342571860}[]{#_Toc340481297}[]{#_Toc342571861}[]{#_Toc340481298}[]{#_Toc342571862}[]{#_Toc340481299}[]{#_Toc342571863}[]{#_Toc340481300}[]{#_Toc342571864}[]{#_Toc340481301}[]{#_Toc342571865}[]{#_Toc340481302}[]{#_Toc342571866}[]{#_Toc340481303}[]{#_Toc342571867}[]{#_Toc340481304}[]{#_Toc342571868}[]{#_Toc329607621}[]{#_Toc329607735}[]{#_Toc330307154}[]{#_Toc329607622}[]{#_Toc329607736}[]{#_Toc330307155}[]{#_Toc340481305}[]{#_Toc342571869}[]{#_Toc340481306}[]{#_Toc342571870}[]{#_Toc340481307}[]{#_Toc342571871}[]{#_Toc340481308}[]{#_Toc342571872}[]{#_Toc340481309}[]{#_Toc342571873}[]{#_Toc340481310}[]{#_Toc342571874}[]{#_Toc340481311}[]{#_Toc342571875}[]{#_Toc340481312}[]{#_Toc342571876}[]{#_Toc340481313}[]{#_Toc342571877}[]{#_Toc340481314}[]{#_Toc342571878}[]{#_Toc340481315}[]{#_Toc342571879}[]{#_Toc340481316}[]{#_Toc342571880}[]{#_Toc340481317}[]{#_Toc342571881}[]{#_Toc340481318}[]{#_Toc342571882}[]{#_Toc340481319}[]{#_Toc342571883}[]{#_Toc340481320}[]{#_Toc342571884}[]{#_Toc340481321}[]{#_Toc342571885}[]{#_Toc340481322}[]{#_Toc342571886}[]{#_Toc340481323}[]{#_Toc342571887}[]{#_Toc340481324}[]{#_Toc342571888}[]{#_Toc340481325}[]{#_Toc342571889}[]{#_Toc340481326}[]{#_Toc342571890}[]{#_Toc340481327}[]{#_Toc342571891}[]{#_Toc340481328}[]{#_Toc342571892}[]{#_Toc340481329}[]{#_Toc342571893}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- link-management periodic-flooding timer**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[link-management periodic-flooding timer]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1750811316}[命令用来设置通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[周期性泛洪]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信息的时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo link-management periodic-flooding timer]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1222922386}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2054191164}

[**[link-management periodic-flooding timer ]{lang="EN-US"}***[interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_319595406}

[**[undo link-management periodic-flooding timer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1855774346}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826386058}

[[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x675014714}[周期性泛洪]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信息的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[180]{lang="FR"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1934545927}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1340329327}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x720806546}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1054730079}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x743808464}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_424326823}

[*[interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826451594}[：通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[周期性泛洪]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信息的时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3600]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2006218234}

[[链路的可预留带宽发生变化时，需要通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_994163710}[泛洪链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[相关信息，以便将变化后的链路情况通知给网络中的设备。通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bandwidth change thresholds]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令可以配置带宽变化达到一定程度时才进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪，而不是只要带宽变化就进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪，以减轻网络负担。此时，没有及时泛洪的链路带宽变化，就可以按照本命令配置的时间间隔周期性地通告给网络中的设备。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果配置时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1161697845}[，则表示关闭周期性泛洪功能。本命令配置的最小生效时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[秒，如果配置的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[29]{lang="EN-US"}[秒，则设备自动将时间间隔设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[执行本命令后，配置的时间间隔立即生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x448691261}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1961851347}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_581653903}[配置通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[周期性泛洪]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信息的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x314366782}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-te\] link-management periodic-flooding timer 100]{lang="EN-US"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- member interface**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[member interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1959531389}[命令用来为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口指定成员接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo member interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1460480999}[命令用来从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口中删除指定的成员接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *tunnel-number*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669312363}[：指定成员接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[tunnel-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的编号，本参数的取值范围与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[load-share ]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_428137716}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[指定成员接口的负载分担权重。设备根据权重确定成员接口转发流量的比例。例如，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口下存在三个成员接口：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel3]{lang="EN-US"}[，负载分担权重分别为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[，则成员接口承担的流量比重分别为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1/4]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1/4]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1/2]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[为负载分担权重，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458454533}

[[成员接口的负载分担权重是相对的，即三个成员接口的负载分担权重分别配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_475441563}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40]{lang="EN-US"}[，与分别配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[相同。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te affinity-attribute**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te affinity]{lang="EN-US"}**[-**attribute**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_312867446}[命令用来配置隧道的亲和属性，即隧道使用的链路需要具有的链路属性。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te affinity]{lang="EN-US"}**[-**attribute**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1558927674}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1964706895}

[**[mpls te affinity-attribute]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *attribute-value* \[ **mask** *mask-value* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826189450}

[**[undo mpls te affinity-attribute]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_43129413}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_605519905}

[[隧道的亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000000]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x877512853}[，掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000000]{lang="EN-US"}[，即隧道可以使用任意属性的链路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_398060443}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x436452961}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1322136625}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787052488}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1017220659}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825730698}

[*[attribute-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2136801668}[：隧道的亲和属性，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000000]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFFFFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[，即为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[位的二进制数。亲和属性中的每一位二进制数代表一种属性，属性值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mask]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *mask-value*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x512650499}[：指定亲和属性的掩码。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mask-value]{lang="EN-US"}*[为亲和属性掩码，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000000]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFFFFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[，即为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[位的二进制数。掩码中的每一位二进制数都表示是否检查该位的链路属性。掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，表示需要检查该位的链路属性，只有该位的链路属性满足一定条件时，才可以使用该链路；掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，表示不检查该位的链路属性，不管该位的链路属性与隧道的亲和属性是否相同，都可以使用该链路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x231943449}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1221503089}[隧道的亲和属性和链路的属性配合，决定了该隧道可以使用哪些链路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[链路属性、亲和属性、亲和属性的掩码都是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_645192963}[位的二进制数。如果希望某条链路能够被隧道所用，则需要满足如下要求：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2070071187}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[的位，亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[的位中链路属性至少有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[位也为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[的位对应的链路属性位不能为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2012899327}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[的位，不对链路属性的相应位进行检查。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[例如，亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFFFFF0]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825796234}[，掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0000FFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[，则可用链路的链路属性高]{style="font-family:宋体"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}[位可以任意取]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[17]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[28]{lang="EN-US"}[位中至少有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，且低]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[位不能为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_230168648}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_263717081}[配置隧道的亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x101]{lang="EN-US"}[，掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x303]{lang="EN-US"}[，即只有链路属性的左数第]{style="font-family:宋体"}[23]{lang="EN-US"}[位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[24]{lang="EN-US"}[位和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[位中至少有一位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[时，才可以使用该链路。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te auto-bandwidth**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te auto-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826254989}[命令用来开启隧道的自动带宽调整和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[或出口速率收集功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

宋体"}

[**[mpls te auto-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **adjustment** \[ **frequency** *seconds* \] \[ **max-bw** *max-bandwidth* \| **min-bw** *min-bandwidth* \] \* \| **collect-bw** \[ **frequency** *seconds* \] }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_717098912}

[**[undo mpls te auto-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1065155847}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1304242599}

[[隧道的自动带宽调整和出口速率收集功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1400262802}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x79800302}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_181323633}

[**[adjustment]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_748547738}[：同时开启隧道的自动带宽调整和出口速率收集功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[collect-bw]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_686602961}[：仅开启隧道的出口速率收集功能，不开启自动带宽调整功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[frequency ]{lang="EN-US"}***[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x870119925}[：指定隧道的自动带宽调整时间间隔（指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[adjustment]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时）或出口速率收集时间间隔（指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[collect-bw]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[300]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[604800]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[86400]{lang="EN-US"}[秒（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[24]{lang="EN-US"}[小时）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[max-bw ]{lang="EN-US"}***[max-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1862670201}[：指定允许调整到的最大带宽值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[max-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则不限制带宽的最大值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[min-bw]{lang="EN-US"}***[ min-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826386061}[：指定允许调整到的最小带宽值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[min-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则不限制带宽的最小值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x674555961}

[[自动带宽调整功能是指设备定时地对隧道的出口速率进行采样，计算采样时间间隔内的平均出口速率；自动带宽调整时间间隔到达后，将隧道的带宽设置为该时间间隔内多次采样中的最大平均出口速率；根据调整后的隧道带宽建立新的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_431579190}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[建立成功后，将流量从旧的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[切换到新的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，并拆除旧的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[出口速率收集功能是指以出口速率收集时间间隔为周期，定期收集隧道上的出口速率，收集的隧道出口速率为在出口速率时间间隔内，多次出口速率采样所获得的最大平均出口速率。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265736786}

[[只有在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x598967316}[视图下通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[auto-bandwidth enable]{lang="FR"}**[命令全局开启自动带宽调整功能，并通过本命令开启指定隧道的自动带宽调整功能或出口速率收集功能后，才能自动对该隧道进行带宽调整或出口速率收集。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_326998819}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[建议配置的自动带宽调整时间间隔大于等于采样时间间隔的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x526578624}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[倍，以便获得准确的出口速率。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在同一个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_990422546}[接口下，自动带宽调整特性与以下命令互斥：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="EN-US"}**[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}**[mpls te route-pinning]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果同时配置了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_405502250}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则仅]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1807452349}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826451597}[开启接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[对应]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的自动带宽调整功能，并配置自动带宽调整的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[小时（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3600]{lang="EN-US"}[秒）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2006414842}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te auto-bandwidth adjustment frequency 3600]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1052240469}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls te tunnel-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x311220907}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[auto-bandwidth enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1612304891}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[reset mpls te auto-bandwidth-adjustment timers]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1394268074}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_992995406}[命令用来关闭接口的自动隧道备份功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265671250}[命令用来开启接口的自动隧道备份功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1786433734}

[**[mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1325215714}

[**[undo mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_266523218}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1673580474}

[[全局开启了自动隧道备份功能后，所有使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x201118545}[能力的接口都会开启自动隧道备份功能，允许自动创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_266588754}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x173181413}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1228042910}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265998931}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1896630148}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1637197721}

[[缺省情况下，全局开启了自动隧道备份功能后，所有使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_266064467}[能力的接口都会开启自动隧道备份功能，允许为采用该接口作为出接口的主隧道建立两条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道（一条链路保护和一条节点保护的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道）。由于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道需要预先建立，占用额外的带宽。因此，为了节省网络带宽资源，应该只对关键的接口进行快速重路由保护，在非关键的接口上可以通过本命令关闭该接口的自动隧道备份功能，避免为该接口建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x636544515}[命令后，已自动创建的保护该接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道会被删除。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265867859}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1185694304}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1230294209}[关闭接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="FR"}[的自动隧道备份功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265933395}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="FR"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[交换应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1548401176}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_568244203}[关闭接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Vlan-interface10]{lang="FR"}[的自动隧道备份功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265736787}

[\[Sysname\] interface vlan-interface 10]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-Vlan-interface10\] mpls te auto-tunnel backup disable]{lang="FR"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_969861038}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265802323}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te backup**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1011608859}[命令用来开启当前隧道的备份功能，并配置使用的备份模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825992845}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_644147951}

[**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **hot-standby** \| **ordinary** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x626442431}

[**[undo mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1483401872}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x320476282}

[[隧道不进行备份。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1567627306}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1094286394}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x164557824}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826058381}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x748968636}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1157122421}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_304453463}

[**[hot-standby]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1771858256}[：热备份，即创建主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[后随即创建备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[失效时，通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[直接将业务切换至备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ordinary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_70356779}[：普通备份，即主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[失效后再创建备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2071746879}

[[通过本命令开启了隧道的备份功能后，将自动启动该隧道的路由记录功能，而不管用户是否配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te record-route]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1568277074}[命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在同一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1442939981}[接口视图下，本命令与以下命令互斥：]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth adjustment]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[如果同时配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1207635858}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则仅]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826123917}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_873029287}[开启隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[的备份功能，并配置使用的备份模式为热备份。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x516351972}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te backup hot-standby]{lang="EN-US"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te backup bandwidth**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te backup bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2067432671}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道可以保护的带宽和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te backup bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1154105991}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826189453}

[**[mpls te backup bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **ct0** \| **ct1** \| **ct2** \| **ct3** \] { *bandwidth* \| **un-limited** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_42932805}

[**[undo mpls te backup bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1911732145}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x641121164}

[[未指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x360020637}[隧道可以保护的带宽和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT]{lang="EN-US"}[类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1398349921}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_858275679}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_608795174}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1465266381}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825730701}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x202309253}

[*[bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1247868295}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道所能保护的带宽总量，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct0]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x36940175}[：只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[可以使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct1]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_445224418}[：只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[可以使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct2]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_194723292}[：只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 2]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[可以使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct3]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1203873056}[：只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 3]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[可以使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[un-limited]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1488252917}[：不对所能保护的带宽总量进行限制，即不能提供带宽保护。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x46300078}

[[如果配置的保护带宽为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[un-limited]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458454534}[，则该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道不能提供带宽保护功能，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[保护时不保证被保护隧道的带宽。如果被保护的隧道流量总和超过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道实际的带宽，会导致被保护隧道所承载的流量丢失。不要求带宽保护的主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[优选此类]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道进行绑定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_475441564}[隧道配置了具体的保护带宽值，则该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道能够提供带宽保护功能。要求带宽保护的主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[优选此类]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道进行绑定。由于带宽为零的隧道意味着尽力转发，占用的带宽不固定，因此，此类]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道不能保护带宽为零的主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。此类]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道也不能保护带宽超过保护带宽的主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x291059314}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825796237}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 3]{lang="EN-US"}[，则该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道可以为所有服务类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[提供保护。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过本命令配置的保护带宽仅用于计算并确立带宽保护关系，在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_230365256}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道上不会预留相应的带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置的保护带宽数目要小于]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1715351159}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道实际的带宽。否则，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[切换后，会导致被保护隧道所承载的流量丢失，并可能造成被保护隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[发生]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458782214}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[切换后，如果修改]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的保护带宽，使得保护带宽类型不同、保护带宽不够或者引起]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[保护类型（是否为主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[提供带宽保护）变化，都将导致主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP Down]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_654020783}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图下执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[后，将自动启动该隧道的路由记录功能，而]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[不管]{style="font-family:宋体"}[用户是否配置了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te record-route]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_397598472}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1623786036}[配置隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[只用来保护属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，并且不对所保护带宽总量进行限制。配置隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="EN-US"}[只用来保护属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，保护的带宽总量是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_234451890}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te backup bandwidth ct0 un-limited]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] quit]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 1]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel1\] mpls te backup bandwidth ct1 1000]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826254988}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls te tunnel-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1047056830}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te fast-reroute]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1988816229}
:::

::: {#1356863026 .myid}
[]{#_Toc404790953}[]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_183852962}[]{#_Toc317060408}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te backup-path**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te backup-path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1147779917}[命令用来配置备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[应用的路径及路径的优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te backup-path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1906550232}[命令用来删除指定的备份路径。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_921510726}

[**[mpls te backup-path preference ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **dynamic** \[ **pce** \[ *ip-address* \]&\<0-8\> \] \| **explicit-path** *path-name* } \[ **no-cspf** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_748459947}

[**[undo mpls te backup-path preference ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1082928403}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826320524}

[[使用自动计算的路径建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x724696839}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_682956898}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1390197350}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x966079186}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_112324635}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_413616879}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_609199254}

[**[preference]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *value*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826386060}[：指定路径的优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[的取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[，数值越小，优先级越高。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[dynamic]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x674490425}[：使用自动计算的路径建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pce]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_202472797}[：使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[计算路径。如果不指定本参数，则本地]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR]{lang="EN-US"}[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[自动计算路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\[ *ip-address* \]&\<0-8\>]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2075701930}[：指定计算路径的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，如果不指定本参数，则自动选择]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<0-8\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[explicit-path]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *path-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_766116480}[：引用已经存在的显式路径，根据该显式路径的要求建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[path-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[为显式路径名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[no-cspf]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832017339}[：不使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[算法计算路径，通过查找路由来计算路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x230552430}

[[一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_720882601}[接口下最多可以配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[条备份路径。不同备份路径的优先级不能相同。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1877210273}[时，按照优先级从高到低的顺序，依次根据配置的路径进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算，直到成功建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果所有路径的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算都失败，则无法建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[只有通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1618556234}[命令开启当前隧道的备份功能后，本命令才会生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_202472800}[地址数目大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[，则会按照配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的顺序发起]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BRPC]{lang="EN-US"}[计算，否则发起]{style="font-family:宋体"}[EPC]{lang="EN-US"}[计算。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果使用该命令或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_202472801}[命令指定了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，则仅与指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话；否则与所有发现的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[建立会话。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1775899208}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_318491535}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[可以使用显式路径]{style="font-family:宋体"}[path1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[计算的路径建立备份]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，且优先使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[计算路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826451596}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te backup-path preference 2 explicit-path path1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te backup-path preference 1 dynamic pce 1.1.1.9 2.2.2.9]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2006349306}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls te tunnel-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1759981625}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1352814768}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_85304259}
:::
