[]{#_Toc404790954}[]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_108642076}[]{#_Toc329607631}[]{#_Toc329607745}[]{#_Toc330307165}[]{#_Toc329607632}[]{#_Toc329607746}[]{#_Toc330307166}[]{#_Toc329607633}[]{#_Toc329607747}[]{#_Toc330307167}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te bandwidth**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1995339500}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道所需的带宽，并指定隧道流量所属的服务类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_265240413}[命令用来恢复缺省配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825992844}

[**[mpls te bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **ct0** \| **ct1** \| **ct2** \| **ct3** \] *bandwidth*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_644082415}

[**[undo mpls te bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1389996822}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x545707080}

[[未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1045312911}[隧道所需的带宽，即带宽为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，隧道流量属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1520910803}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1436299960}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x588626532}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1361194592}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826058380}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x749034172}

[**[ct0]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_57490940}[：配置隧道流量属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct1]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1671601879}[：配置隧道流量属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct2]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x781906296}[：配置隧道流量属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 2]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ct3]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x595186037}[：配置隧道流量属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 3]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_980946648}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道所需的带宽，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1771230660}

[[执行本命令时，如果不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ct0]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826123916}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ct1]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ct2]{lang="EN-US"}**[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ct3]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则隧道流量属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[当配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_873094823}[隧道所需的带宽大于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1024kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[时，建议配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1024kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[的整数倍。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令只用于采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP-TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x199699291}[协议建立的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。使用静态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="PT-BR"}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道时，隧道所需的带宽及隧道流量所属的服务类型由]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static-cr-lsp ingress]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令决定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1733096197}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_154936952}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道所需带宽为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，隧道流量所属的服务类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CT 1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_571694441}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te bandwidth ct1 1000]{lang="EN-US"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te bandwidth change thresholds**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te bandwidth change thresholds]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1614886903}[命令用来配置通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信息的带宽变化阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ]{lang="EN-US"}[mpls te bandwidth change thresholds]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x675861229}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1531277719}

[**[mpls te bandwidth change thresholds]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **down** \| **up** } *percent*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x68426958}

[**[undo mpls te bandwidth change thresholds]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **down** \| **up** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825730700}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x202374789}

[[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x440286347}[泛洪]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信息的带宽变化阈值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10%]{lang="EN-US"}[，即可预留带宽增加或减少]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10%]{lang="EN-US"}[时进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1343247622}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x779223793}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1462466160}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_126291688}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_690687567}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1848226189}

[**[down]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825796236}[：配置可预留带宽减少时]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[进行泛洪的百分比阈值。当链路可预留带宽的减少值与链路原有可预留带宽的比值大于等于此百分比阈值时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[将进行泛洪，并更新流量工程数据库。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[up]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_230299720}[：配置可预留带宽增加时]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[进行泛洪的百分比阈值。当链路可预留带宽的增加值与链路原有可预留带宽的比值大于等于此百分比阈值时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[将进行泛洪，并更新流量工程数据库。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[percent]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x673745935}[：带宽变化的百分比阈值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_567631983}

[[链路的可预留带宽发生变化时，需要通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x33813841}[泛洪链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[相关信息，以便将变化后的链路情况通知给网络中的设备。但是，频繁地]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪会增加网络中的流量，加大网络中设备的处理负担，并导致]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上频繁进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算，占用过多的系统资源。通过本命令可以配置带宽变化达到一定程度时才进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪，以减轻网络负担。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1107003113}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_59486030}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1463795456}[在接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上配置链路带宽减少]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100%]{lang="EN-US"}[时进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826254991}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] ]{lang="EN-US"}[mpls te ]{lang="EN-US"}[bandwidth change ]{lang="EN-US"}[thresholds down 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[交换应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1047515583}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x591505197}[在接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Vlan-interface10]{lang="EN-US"}[上配置链路带宽减少]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100%]{lang="EN-US"}[时进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[泛洪。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_859210717}

[\[Sysname\] interface vlan-interface 10]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Vlan-interface10\] ]{lang="EN-US"}[mpls te ]{lang="EN-US"}[bandwidth change ]{lang="EN-US"}[thresholds down 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x440277258}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[link-management periodic-flooding timer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1759300000}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te bidirectional**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1764997271}[命令用来在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道接口上启用双向隧道功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826320527}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x724631303}

[**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **associated reverse-lsp** { **lsp-name** *lsp-name* \| **lsr-id** *ingress-lsr-id* **tunnel-id** *tunnel-id* } \| **co-routed**  { **active** \| **passive** **reverse-lsp lsr-id** *ingress-lsr-id* **tunnel-id** *tunnel-id* } }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x550603212}

[**[undo mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x280587014}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1445700253}

[[未启用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_306135930}[隧道接口的双向隧道功能，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道接口上建立的隧道为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[单向隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x201712980}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x642054057}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_705641978}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826386063}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x674687033}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_754862534}

[**[associated reverse-lsp lsp-name]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *lsp-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2138125484}[：配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Associated]{lang="EN-US"}[方式的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道，并指定关联的反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[lsp-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[为反向静态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[15]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[associated reverse-lsp lsr-id]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ingress-lsr-id* **tunnel-id** *tunnel-id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1963836748}[：配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Associated]{lang="EN-US"}[方式的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道，并指定关联的反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ingress-lsr-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的入节点]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[tunnel-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1484000874}[：配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}[方式的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[active]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_729249655}[：指定本端作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}[方式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道的主动方。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[passive]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **reverse-lsp lsr-id** *ingress-lsr-id* **tunnel-id** *tunnel-id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1861298036}[：指定本端作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}[方式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道的被动方，并指定关联的反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ingress-lsr-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的入节点]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[tunnel-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。被动方上需要指定反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，以便将正、反两个方向的单向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[关联为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x445665485}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826451599}[双向隧道的建立有如下几种方式：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2006545914}[方式：通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信令协议建立]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道。配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}[方式]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道时，隧道的两端节点需要分别配置为主动方（]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Active]{lang="EN-US"}[）和被动方（]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Passive]{lang="EN-US"}[）。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Associated]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1817796242}[方式：通过配置手工将两条单向的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[绑定，从而形成]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道。绑定在一起的两条单向]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[可以通过不同的方式建立，例如一个方向上的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[使用静态方式建立，而另一个方向上的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[信令建立。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[绑定在一起的两条单向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[使用的路径可以不同。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x649400834}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[建立]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2118774448}[方式]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道时，必须配置建立隧道使用的信令协议为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_992928173}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道时，在隧道两端都需要关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PHP]{lang="EN-US"}[功能，为倒数第二跳分配非空标签。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1568170592}[与]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="FR"}**[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te fast-reroute]{lang="EN-US"}**[同时配置，则仅]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1671677506}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1094210467}[方式]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[ # ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825992847}[在隧道本端接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[上启用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道功能，配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}[方式的主动方]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x763746997}[在隧道对端接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="EN-US"}[上启用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[双向隧道功能，并配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Co-routed]{lang="EN-US"}[方式双向隧道的被动方，关联的反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的入节点]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10.0.0.1]{lang="EN-US"}[，反向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel ID]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te enable (IS-IS view)**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832607164}[命令用来在当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程下使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x313346302}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832672700}

[**[mpls te enable ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **level-1** \| **level-2** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_59047439}

[**[undo mpls te enable ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **level-1** \| **level-2** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1971625749}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832082873}

[[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_889978454}[进程的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_941376667}

[[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832148409}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1831951801}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1790156633}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1617542716}

[**[level-1]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832017337}[：在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS Level-1]{lang="EN-US"}[使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[level-2]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_75020107}[：在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1831820729}

[[如果不指定级别，则同时使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x969206763}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[IS-IS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1846008894}[使用扩展]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS]{lang="EN-US"}[可达性]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[（类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[22]{lang="EN-US"}[）的子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[属性信息，扩展]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS]{lang="EN-US"}[可达性]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[wide]{lang="EN-US"}[类型的开销值。因此，配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[时，必须配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[的开销值类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[wide]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[compatible]{lang="EN-US"}**[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[wide-compatible]{lang="EN-US"}**[。有关]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[的介绍请参见"三层技术]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由配置指导"中的"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1831886265}[视图下重复执行本命令，新的配置覆盖已有配置。例如，执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令后执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te enable level-1]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，则使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力，关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_992684771}[命令同时使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力后，如果配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo mpls te enable level-1]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，则仅关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力仍然使能。配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo mpls te enable level-2]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，与此类似。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1831689657}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x981973743}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Level-2]{lang="EN-US"}[使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1923141808}

[\[Sysname\] isis 1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-isis-1\] cost-style compatible]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-isis-1\] mpls te enable level-2]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1831755193}

[**[bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x318020936}[：指定主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[需要进行带宽保护。如果不指定本参数，则表示主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[不需要进行带宽保护。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1091410937}

[[FRR]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_664176061}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Fast Reroute]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[快速重路由]{style="font-family:宋体"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[中实现网络局部保护的技术。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="FR"}[的切换速度可以达到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[50ms]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[能够最大程度减少网络故障时数据的丢失。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[开启隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1126816438}[功能后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[当主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[上的某条链路或某个节点失效时，流量会被切换到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道上。同时，隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点尝试建立新的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。新的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[建立成功后，流量将切换到新的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[不需要进行带宽保护时，主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458585604}[优选不提供保护带宽的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，表明]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[切换后不要求带宽保证；需要进行带宽保护时，主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[优选具有足够保护带宽的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，以提供]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FRR]{lang="EN-US"}[切换后的带宽保证。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x750425327}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过本命令开启了隧道的快速重路由功能后]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_748530563}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[将自动启动该隧道的标签记录功能]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[而不管用户是否配置了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te record-route label]{lang="FR"}**[命令。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[无论主]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x295744968}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[是否要求带宽保护，一旦主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[和提供保护带宽的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道绑定，就会占用保护带宽，在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PLR]{lang="EN-US"}[节点的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RRO]{lang="EN-US"}[信息中可以看到带宽保护标记。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果同时配置了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te fast-reroute]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_539588906}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则仅]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="EN-US"}**[生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1047581119}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_44221724}[开启接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[的快速重路由功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x572781387}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te fast-reroute]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x347763186}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls te tunnel-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x643392290}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te backup bandwidth]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2048907315}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te fast-reroute bypass-tunnel**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te fast-reroute bypass-tunnel]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1049505991}[命令用来为被保护的接口（当前接口）指定一条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te fast-reroute bypass-tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458454532}[命令用来删除指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_475441562}

[**[mpls te fast-reroute bypass-tunnel]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x291059316}[ ]{lang="FR"}**[tunnel]{lang="FR"}**[ *tunnel-number*]{lang="FR"}

[**[undo ]{lang="ES"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1098511411}**[mpls te fast-reroute bypass-tunnel]{lang="FR"}**[ ]{lang="FR"}**[tunnel]{lang="FR"}**[ *tunnel-number*]{lang="FR"}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458782212}

[[接口上不存在任何]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x399411246}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1502629490}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458847748}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1860021668}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1250417698}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458257929}

[**[tunnel]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_248984141}*[ tunnel-number]{lang="FR"}*[：指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道对应的隧道接口的编号。不同型号的设备支持的取值范围不同，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1583472261}

[[执行本命令后，如果主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458323465}[的出接口（当前接口）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Down]{lang="EN-US"}[、通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BFD]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hello]{lang="EN-US"}[机制检测到邻居故障，则会将主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文切换到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道进行转发。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[通过多次执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_977286423}**[mpls te]{lang="FR"}[ fast-reroute bypass-tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，可以为一个接口指定多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。最多可以为一个被保护接口指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[一条隧道最多可以保护]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2061818363}[个接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1268391020}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_458126857}[隧道必须使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP]{lang="EN-US"}[信令协议建立。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[被保护的接口不能是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1152205359}[隧道的出接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te igp advertise**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826320526}[命令用来开启转发邻接功能，即将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路发布到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由中。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x724565767}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2120506515}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_792580200}[接口视图：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **hold-time** *value* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_674383988}

[**[undo mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_849821891}

[[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_792645736}[接口视图：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_245671125}

[**[undo mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1635802151}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x650457695}

[[转发邻接功能处于关闭状态，即不会将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_998228564}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路发布到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由中。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1335108537}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826386062}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x674621497}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1941467602}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1903659801}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1951777509}

[**[hold-time ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x616169159}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道状态发生变化，即由]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态变更为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[状态或由]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[状态变更为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态后，通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[发布该信息的等待时间。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为毫秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[毫秒，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道状态变化后，立即通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[发布该信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1112015441}

[[转发邻接功能是将流量引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x357866758}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[的一种方式。该功能将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[当作一条直接连接隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="FR"}[节点和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Egress]{lang="FR"}[节点的链路，通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[路由协议将该链路发布到网络中，以便网络中的节点在路由计算时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1246611610}[和转发邻接功能的区别在于：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826451598}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能后，只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点计算]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由时会考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能不会通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由协议将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路发布出去。因此，其他设备在路由计算时不会考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2006480378}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上开启转发邻接功能后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点会通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由协议将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路发布出去。因此，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[网络中的所有设备在路由计算时都会考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1332865044}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[使用转发邻接功能时，需要在隧道两端节点上都建立到达对方的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1155519200}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，并在两端节点上都开启转发邻接功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果在同一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x693021525}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[/Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="FR"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[下重复执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te igp advertise]{lang="FR"}**[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="FR"}**[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[则新的配置覆盖原有配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x357597084}[接口成为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="FR"}[接口的成员接口后，该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口下的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[hold-time]{lang="FR"}**[配置仍然可以生效。建议在该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[下使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令取消]{style="font-family:宋体"}[hold-time]{lang="EN-US"}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[避免]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="FR"}[接口的状态受到影响。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x362565465}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1477679058}[开启转发邻接功能，并配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道状态变化后通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[发布该信息的等待时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000]{lang="EN-US"}[毫秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te igp metric**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te igp metric]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_434135188}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te igp metric]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_524264279}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x203339175}

[**[mpls te igp metric]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **absolute** *value* \| **relative** *value* }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1112148603}

[**[undo mpls te igp metric]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_781691358}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826058382}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x748903100}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的度量值等于其]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x736912105}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x158151635}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1513982025}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1962607928}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1893172165}

[**[absolute]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1353805658}[：以绝对值的方式指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的度量值，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的度量值为配置的值]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[正整数，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[relative]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x826123918}[：以相对值的方式指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的度量值，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的度量值为配置的值]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[+MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[可以是正整数、负整数或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-10]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_873225895}

[[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1257558976}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="FR"}[功能时]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="FR"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路参与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[路由的计算。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道这条链路在路由计算过程中的度量值可以通过本命令来配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1174325569}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1353739527}[配置在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能中计算]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[的度量值为该隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1502852783}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te igp metric relative -1]{lang="EN-US"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te igp shortcut**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1231831629}[命令用来开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能，即在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道当作一条链路参与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的计算。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x138036428}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x262211461}

[**[mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **isis** \| **ospf** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_694850871}

[**[undo mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2128005731}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1958857105}

[[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2002521140}[功能处于关闭状态，即在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由计算时不考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825730702}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x202505861}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1190315956}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1300618192}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1790429319}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1542209206}

[**[isis]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2129485194}[：指定在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的路由计算中考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ospf]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x804533749}[：指定在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的路由计算中考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_480279724}

[[IGP Shortcut]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x825796238}[功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[是将流量引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="FR"}[捆绑隧道]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[的一种方式。该功能将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="FR"}[捆绑隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[当作一条直接连接隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="FR"}[节点和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Egress]{lang="FR"}[节点的链路，在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="FR"}[节点上]{style="font-family:宋体"}[进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[路由计算时考虑该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="FR"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_229906504}[和转发邻接功能的区别在于：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_649701889}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能后，只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点计算]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由时会考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能不会通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由协议将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路发布出去。因此，其他设备在路由计算时不会考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1491768921}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上开启转发邻接功能后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点会通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由协议将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道作为一条链路发布出去。因此，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[网络中的所有设备在路由计算时都会考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x408702090}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行本命令时如果不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x592312232}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的路由计算中都考虑]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[捆绑隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果在同一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1996407638}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[/Tunnel-Bundle]{lang="EN-US"}[接口下重复执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te igp advertise]{lang="FR"}**[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="FR"}**[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[则新的配置覆盖原有配置。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1506246978}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096059314}[开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP Shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}[功能，在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上将该隧道当作一条链路参与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的计算]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x553435335}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te igp shortcut]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1562487684}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te igp advertise]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x103775348}

[[链路的属性值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000000]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1732775879}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2094255531}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2052216390}

[*[attribute-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2015777155}[：链路的属性，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000000]{lang="EN-US"}[到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFFFFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[，即为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[位的二进制数，每一位二进制数代表一个属性，属性值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x924965843}

[[设备通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1136745496}[发布的链路]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[相关信息中包括本命令配置的链路属性。隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点获取到该信息后，根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点上配置的隧道亲和属性，判断建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道时是否可以使用该链路。如果希望某条链路能够被隧道所用，则需要满足如下要求：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_235581590}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[的位，亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[的位中链路属性至少有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[位也为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[的位对应的链路属性位不能为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_823054465}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[的位，不对链路属性的相应位进行检查。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[例如，亲和属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFFFFF0]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1144019950}[，掩码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0000FFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[，则可用链路的链路属性高]{style="font-family:宋体"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}[位可以任意取]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[17]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[28]{lang="EN-US"}[位中至少有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[，且低]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[位不能为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1191223988}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1095862706}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2030211473}[在接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上配置该链路的属性为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00000101]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[链路的最大可预留带宽]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[bandwidth-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1989847974}[不能大于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te metric**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te metric]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1312681145}[命令用来配置链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **mpls te metric**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1184434624}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_533104751}

[**[mpls te metric]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1786862011}[ *value*]{lang="FR"}

[**[undo mpls te metric]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1095862704}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2030342545}

[[链路使用其]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x662824089}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[的度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_777516091}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1003952992}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x307079793}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_286853608}

[*[value]{lang="FR"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096321456}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[取值范围是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="FR"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2147032014}

[[设备在发布的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1832421516}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[路由中携带链路的两种度量值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值。其中，链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值可以通过本命令来配置。隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点获取到链路的度量值后，根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}[节点设备上]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**[命令或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**[命令的配置，决定选择路径时采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值还是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_80584699}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1607186858}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x278646816}[在接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上配置链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[度量值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te path**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x233183035}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[应用的路径及路径的优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x197962070}[命令用来删除指定的路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096190384}

[**[mpls te path preference ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value ]{lang="EN-US"}*[{ **dynamic** \[ **pce** \[ *ip-address* \]&\<0-8\> \] \| **explicit-path** *path-name* } \[ **no-cspf** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1772463994}

[**[undo mpls te path preference ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1351482450}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_872096752}

[[使用自动计算的路径建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1658182134}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1975895108}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1070583960}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x862541575}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x628189757}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096124848}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_612510462}

[**[preference]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *value*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x899726452}[：指定路径的优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[的取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[，数值越小，优先级越高。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[dynamic]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2092568443}[：使用自动计算的路径建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pce]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800249}[：使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[计算路径。如果不指定本参数，则本地]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR]{lang="EN-US"}[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[自动计算路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\[ *ip-address* \]&\<0-8\>]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800252}[：指定计算路径的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，如果不指定本参数，则自动选择]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<0-8\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[explicit-path]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *path-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1848268933}[：引用已经存在的显式路径，根据该显式路径的要求建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[path-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[为显式路径名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[no-cspf]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x896800479}[：不使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[算法计算路径，通过查找路由来计算路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x321007637}

[[一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_787909594}[接口下最多可以配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[条路径。不同路径的优先级不能相同。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1402435926}[时，按照优先级从高到低的顺序，依次根据配置的路径进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算，直到成功建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果所有路径的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算都失败，则无法建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800256}[地址数目大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[，则会按照配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的顺序发起]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BRPC]{lang="EN-US"}[计算，否则发起]{style="font-family:宋体"}[EPC]{lang="EN-US"}[计算。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果使用该命令或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup-path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800258}[命令指定了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，则仅与指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话；否则与所有发现的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[建立会话。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_998926959}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096583600}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[可以使用显式路径]{style="font-family:宋体"}[path1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[计算的路径建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，且优先使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[计算路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1354103353}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te path preference 2 explicit-path path1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te path preference 1 dynamic pce 1.1.1.9 2.2.2.9]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1558264026}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls te tunnel-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_724508833}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te backup-path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1553805531}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te path-metric-type**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1417664584}[命令用来配置为当前隧道选路时使用的链路度量值类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1287247703}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096518064}

[**[mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x537414868}[ { **igp** \| **te** }]{lang="FR"}

[**[undo mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_766185516}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1686864470}

[[没有指定当前隧道选路时使用的链路度量值类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1192184028}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1384590072}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2124843318}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1325981117}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x164700165}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096059313}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x553500871}

[**[igp]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x778898834}[：使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[度量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[te]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1859280429}[：使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="EN-US"}[度量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2126783825}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2111145235}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[中每条链路都具有两种度量值：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值。通过合理地规划两种度量值，可以实现为不同种类的业务选择不同的隧道。例如，使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值来表示链路延迟的大小（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值越小，链路的延迟越小），使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值来表示链路带宽的大小（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值越小，链路的带宽越大）。建立两条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="FR"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel2]{lang="FR"}[），分别用来承载语音业务和视频业务。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="FR"}[选择路径时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值，可以实现为延迟要求较高的语音业务选择延迟小的路径；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel2]{lang="FR"}[选择路径时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值，可以实现为数据量较大的视频业务选择带宽大的路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1302943249}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口视图下执行了本命令，则该隧道使用本接口下配置的度量值类型选择路径；否则，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[视图下]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**[命令配置的度量值类型选择路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1326427692}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1963278648}[配置为隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="FR"}[选路时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1095993777}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te path-metric-type igp]{lang="FR"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te metric]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_773556916}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[path-metric-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_725297992}
:::

[]{#_Toc404790974}[]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1249755981}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te priority**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **priority**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_413479201}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的建立优先级和保持优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te priority]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1942153650}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1095928241}

[**[mpls te priority]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *setup-priority* \[ *hold-priority* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_359051954}

[**[undo mpls te priority]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1301034784}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x520292786}

[[建立优先级和保持优先级都为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[7]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1009707491}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_471957843}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1879217439}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1700758451}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1095862705}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2030408081}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1630590700}

[*[setup-priority]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1610856625}[：建立优先级，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[7]{lang="EN-US"}[，数值越小优先级越高。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[hold-priority]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_353037652}[：保持优先级，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[7]{lang="EN-US"}[，数值越小优先级越高。如果不指定本参数，则保持优先级与建立优先级相同。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_399778563}

[[建立优先级和保持优先级标识了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x639062498}[隧道的重要程度。数值越小，优先级越高，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道越重要。如果一条隧道的建立优先级数值小于另一条隧道的保持优先级，则该隧道可以抢占另一条隧道的资源。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[建立优先级和保持优先级可以应用在以下场景：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1650996515}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1279990205}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道经过相同的路径，而该路径上的带宽不足以满足所有隧道的带宽要求时，通过为不同的隧道配置不同的建立优先级和保持优先级，可以确保重要的隧道能够优先建立。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在重要的隧道建立之前，网络中已经建立了多条]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096321457}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，占用了带宽资源，使得重要的隧道无法使用最优路径建立。此时，通过为重要的隧道配置更高的优先级，可以使得该隧道抢占已建立隧道的资源，采用最优路径建立重要隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，隧道的建立优先级不能高于该隧道的保持优先级，即其在数值上应大于或等于保持优先级。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2147097550}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1720976875}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_119490951}[配置隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[的建立优先级和保持优先级都为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x641384940}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te priority 1 1]{lang="FR"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1618427548}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls te tunnel-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1744468279}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te record-route**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te record-route]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096255921}[命令用来开启隧道的路由记录或标签记录功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te record-route]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_216512190}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1420575867}

[**[mpls te record-route]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x374454959}[ \[ **label** \]]{lang="FR"}

[[隧道的路由记录和标签记录功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1184652437}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2143081238}

[[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1358609969}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096190385}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1772398458}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_200067850}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1370799235}

[**[label]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_364649270}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[同时开启路由记录和标签记录功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。如果不指定本参数，则仅开启路由记录功能，未开启标签记录功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1569608353}

[[路由记录和标签记录功能用来记录]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2014859602}[隧道经过的各个节点及各个节点分配的标签值，以便用户根据记录的信息了解]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道经过的路径和标签分配情况。在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道出现故障时，用户也可以根据记录的信息对故障进行定位。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1957430282}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te reoptimization (Tunnel Interface view)**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1729797745}[命令用来开启隧道重优化功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te reoptimization]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1606488174}[命令用来关闭隧道重优化功能。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1794258884}

[**[mpls te reoptimization ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **frequency** *seconds* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x47169635}

[**[undo mpls te reoptimization]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096583601}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1354037817}

[[隧道重优化功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1209332947}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x472630573}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1461387970}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1431798827}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x217269868}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1356005938}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_693952011}

[**[frequency]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *seconds*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096518065}[：指定隧道重优化频率。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[604800]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3600]{lang="EN-US"}[秒，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[小时。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x537349332}

[[隧道重优化功能是指周期性地或通过在用户视图下执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_778113675}**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="FR"}**[命令手工触发隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="FR"}[节点重新选择路径]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[以便将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道切换到当前的最优路径。例如，如果在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道建立时，最优路径上的链路没有足够的可预留带宽，则会导致]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道未使用最优路径建立。通过隧道重优化功能，可以实现链路上具有足够的带宽时将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道自动切换到最优路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在同一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_586958597}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口下]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[隧道重优化功能与以下命令互斥]{style="font-family:宋体"}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth adjustment]{lang="FR"}**[，]{style="font-family:
宋体"}**[mpls te route-pinning]{lang="FR"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te backup]{lang="FR"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果同时配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1026757179}**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="FR"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="FR"}**[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[则仅]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te bidirectional]{lang="FR"}**[生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1259113015}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1656830174}[开启隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="FR"}[的重优化功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[并配置每隔]{style="font-family:宋体"}[43200]{lang="FR"}[秒]{style="font-family:宋体"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[12]{lang="FR"}[小时]{style="font-family:宋体"}[）]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[重新计算路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te reoptimization frequency 43200]{lang="EN-US"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te reoptimization (User view)**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2039051075}[命令用来立即对所有开启了重优化功能的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS ]{lang="EN-US"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道进行重优化。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1221905242}

[**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1645493362}

[[用户视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x947506786}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te resv-style**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te resv-style]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_358593197}[命令用来配置隧道的资源预留风格]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1692983932}[ **mpls te resv-style**]{lang="PT-BR"}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_46463413}

[**[mpls te resv-style]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_432973842}[ { **ff** \| **se** }]{lang="FR"}

[**[undo]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_587056152}[ **mpls te resv-style**]{lang="FR"}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x757747765}

[[隧道的资源预留风格为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_21873264}[SE]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x709406585}

[[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1095862702}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2029949329}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1495836016}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1207910618}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x250404156}

[**[ff]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_64561184}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FF]{lang="FR"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Fixed-Filter]{lang="FR"}[，固定过滤器）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[资源预留风格。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[se]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1707063622}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SE]{lang="FR"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Shared-Explicit]{lang="FR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[共享显式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[资源预留风格。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_803581776}

[[FF]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1006424541}[资源预留风格是指为每个发送者单独预留资源，同一会话中的不同发送者不能共享资源。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[SE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096321454}[资源预留风格是指为同一个会话中的不同发送者预留同一个资源，不同发送者之间可以共享资源。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2147163086}[只有采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RSVP-TE]{lang="PT-BR"}[协议建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道时]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[本命令才会生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x696814871}

[[\# ]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_187169043}[配置使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FF]{lang="PT-BR"}[资源预留风格建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="PT-BR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="PT-BR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname\] interface tunnel 0 mode mpls-te]{lang="PT-BR"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te resv-style ff]{lang="FR"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te retry**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te retry]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_215922367}[命令用来配置尝试建立隧道的最大次数。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_966596211}

[[尝试建立隧道的最大次数为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1667081231}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_337212472}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1048749517}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_646339817}

[*[times]{lang="FR"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_955683025}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[尝试建立隧道的最大次数]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="FR"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4294967295]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_883311525}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_886892479}[隧道建立失败后，隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="FR"}[节点等待]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te timer retry]{lang="FR"}**[命令配置的隧道重建时间间隔后]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，将尝试重新建立隧道，直到隧道建立成功或尝试建立隧道的次数达到本命令配置的值。如果尝试建立隧道的次数达到本命令配置的值时仍未成功建立隧道，则等待较长的一段时间后，重复上述过程。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x274448161}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096124846}[配置尝试建立隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="EN-US"}[的最大次数为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname-Tunnel0\] mpls te retry 20]{lang="FR"}

[[路由固定功能是指]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1991798032}[创建成功后，该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[不随路由变化而变化。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在路由变化频繁的网络中，如果不希望]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x249318537}[随着路由频繁变化，则可以通过本功能确保只要已建立的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[可用就不重新创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在同一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1816349240}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口视图下，本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[命令与以下命令互斥]{style="font-family:宋体"}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te reoptimization]{lang="FR"}**[命令和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te auto-bandwidth adjustment]{lang="FR"}**[命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1599842823}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1083076208}[开启隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="FR"}[的路由固定功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[lsp-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1234820849}[：引用的静态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[的名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[15]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x656600041}

[[只有在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_568914638}[接口视图下配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te signaling static]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，本命令才会生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令需要在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096255919}[节点上执行，即本命令中引用的静态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[，必须是通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static-cr-lsp ingress]{lang="FR"}**[命令创建的。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- mpls te timer retry**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls te timer retry]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x990416642}[命令用来配置隧道重建的时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls te timer retry]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x678621345}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_116166185}

[**[mpls te timer retry]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *seconds*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_373868576}

[**[undo mpls te timer retry]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1685898713}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1334956434}

[[隧道重建的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096124847}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_613362430}

[[Tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_567747588}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2086239665}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_882073547}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x692719730}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2021461201}

[*[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1240556418}[：隧道重建的时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[604800]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1562419192}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096583599}[隧道建立失败后，隧道的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ingress]{lang="FR"}[节点等待本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[命令配置的隧道重建时间间隔后]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，将尝试重新建立隧道，直到隧道建立成功或尝试建立隧道的次数达到]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te retry]{lang="FR"}**[命令配置的值。如果尝试建立隧道的次数达到最大值时仍未成功建立隧道，则等待较长的一段时间后，重复上述过程。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x984090046}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1445247998}[配置每隔]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20]{lang="FR"}[秒重建隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel0]{lang="FR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}
