**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- nexthop**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[nexthop]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1096518063}[命令用来在显式路径中添加或修改节点及其属性（显式路径中是否包括添加的节点；添加的节点是松散下一跳，还是严格下一跳）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo nexthop]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x536956116}[命令用来删除显式路径中的指定节点。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1962801600}

[**[nexthop]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **index** *index-number* \] *ip-address* \[ **exclude** \| **include** \[ **loose** \| **strict** \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1507575647}

[**[undo nexthop index ]{lang="EN-US"}***[index-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_165833289}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1536965620}

[[显式路径中不存在任何节点。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1184746734}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1914462282}

[[显式路径视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_30415731}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1632824041}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1480956562}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_58319023}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_79425033}

[*[index-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1243824459}[：节点在显式路径中的索引，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定本参数，则自动计算索引值，大小为当前最大索引值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[+100]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x239164561}[：显式路径中节点的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，点分十进制格式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[exclude]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1380519838}[：在显式路径中不能包括此节点。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[include]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1801238895}[：指定在显式路径中需要包含此节点。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[loose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1632889577}[：指定的节点为松散下一跳，即上一跳与本节点可以不是直接相连。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[strict]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1460994351}[：指定的节点为严格下一跳，即上一跳与本节点必须直接相连。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1498704305}

[[本命令中指定的节点地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1686706773}[可以是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1557503937}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址：即设备上接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，代表该地址所属的设备。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[设备的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x143479300}[LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[：代表该设备。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[严格下一跳的地址只能是链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_85338817}[地址。松散下一跳的地址可以是链路的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址和设备的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[对于]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[exclude]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_719753620}[的节点，在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算时排除该地址对应的链路或设备；对于]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[include]{lang="EN-US"}**[的节点，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[计算时按照索引从小到大的顺序，依次查找符合节点地址要求的链路，以便建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CRLSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1174808506}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1632955113}**[nexthop]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令时，如果指定了索引，且该索引已经存在，则修改节点的地址或属性。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果没有指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[include]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1973281578}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[exclude]{lang="EN-US"}**[关键字，则缺省为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[include]{lang="EN-US"}**[，即在显式路径中需要包含此]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[节点]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果没有指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1257881775}**[loose]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[strict]{lang="EN-US"}**[关键字，则缺省为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[strict]{lang="EN-US"}**[，即上一跳与本节点必须直接相连。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x298209421}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_721554446}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[显式路径中不包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10.0.0.125]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_718847342}

[\[Sysname\] explicit-path path1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-explicit-path-path1\] next-hop 10.0.0.125 exclude]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x302782357}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display explicit-path]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_911149132}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- nhop-only**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[nhop-only]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_693154284}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[仅自动创建链路保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo nhop-only]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x896866020}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1468469217}

[**[nhop-only]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x897062628}

[**[undo nhop-only]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1220658163}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1218701052}

[[链路保护和节点保护的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x896997092}[隧道都会自动创建。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1225745618}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x897193700}[自动隧道备份视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1958164527}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x897128164}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1598390076}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x896276196}

[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x481882448}**[nhop-only]{lang="FR"}**[命令后，如果设备已创建节点保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道，则节点保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道将被删除。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x896210660}**[undo nhop-only]{lang="FR"}**[命令后，设备将恢复创建节点保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_890637899}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[仅自动创建链路保护类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- path-metric-type**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1633020649}[命令用来配置未配置度量类型的隧道选路时使用的链路度量值类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1284325155}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1903696655}

[**[path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1938289447}[ { **igp** \| **te** }]{lang="FR"}

[**[undo path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2056329}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x368301711}

[[未配置度量类型的隧道选路时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x717908609}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_917665753}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_59021681}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1632561897}

[[network-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2094529300}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1579797796}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_49868296}

[**[igp]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1643329229}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[te]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2073928343}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2046648491}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_805428328}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[中每条链路都具有两种度量值：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值。通过合理地规划两种度量值，可以实现为不同种类的业务选择不同的隧道。例如，使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值来表示链路延迟的大小（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值越小，链路的延迟越小），使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值来表示链路带宽的大小（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值越小，链路的带宽越大）。建立两条]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="FR"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel2]{lang="FR"}[），分别用来承载语音业务和视频业务。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel1]{lang="FR"}[选择路径时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值，可以实现为延迟要求较高的语音业务选择延迟小的路径；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Tunnel2]{lang="FR"}[选择路径时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[度量值，可以实现为数据量较大的视频业务选择带宽大的路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_729932051}[Tunnel]{lang="FR"}[接口视图下执行了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="FR"}**[命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，则该隧道使用接口下配置的度量值类型选择路径；否则，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使用本命令配置的度量值类型选择路径。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1632627433}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_96423584}[对所有未指定度量类型的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS ]{lang="EN-US"}[TE]{lang="FR"}[隧道]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[配置其在进行路径计算时使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="FR"}[度量值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1665869348}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-te\] path-metric-type]{lang="EN-US"}[ igp]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mpls te path-metric-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1555318319}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1367179843}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1371505220}

[*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1371505218}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1371505216}

[[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1371505217}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址后，本设备即可作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[建议配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1371505214}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Loopbac]{lang="EN-US"}[k]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，可在该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Loopbac]{lang="EN-US"}[k]{lang="EN-US"}[接口上使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[ TE]{lang="EN-US"}[来发布]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息，使]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[或其他]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[自动发现本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[；或在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[设备上静态指定本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_213286329}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，则本设备仅可作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[，并使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR ID]{lang="EN-US"}[与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[通信。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[只能向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[发起]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[连接请求，不接受]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[连接请求。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_213286332}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_213286336}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10.10.10.10]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- pce deadtimer**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[pce deadtimer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2125365830}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话的保持时间。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo pce deadtimer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2125365828}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2125365826}

[**[pce deadtimer ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2125365827}

[**[undo pce deadtimer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2125365825}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2125365822}

[[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x169050694}[会话的保持时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[120]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x169050695}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x169050693}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x169050691}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x169050688}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x169050686}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787264442}

[*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787264441}[：对等体之间的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话保持时间，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[表示与对等体之间的会话不会超时。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787264443}

[[配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[deadtimer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787264446}[值会通告给对等体，对等体使用该值作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话的保持时间。如果]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[deadtimer]{lang="EN-US"}**[内没有收到对等体发送的任何]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[消息，则断开]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[会话中断后，对等体之间会尝试重新建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[deadtimer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787264447}[值必须大于]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**[值，否则会导致会话中断。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1787264449}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_977960378}[配置对等体之间的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话保持时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[180]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- pce keepalive**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[pce keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1360691783}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的发送时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo pce keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1360691781}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2123203134}

[**[pce keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}***[ interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166888006}

[**[undo pce keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166888004}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166888005}

[[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166888002}[消息的发送时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166888000}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166887998}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x166887999}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1789427130}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1789427131}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1789427133}

[*[interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1789427135}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的发送时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话建立后不再发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1789427138}

[[对等体之间建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1789427137}[会话时，如果本端配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的发送时间间隔小于对端的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[min-keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则本端会将对端配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[min-keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**[作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的发送时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果本端配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_980123065}[消息的发送时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，则对端的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[min-keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**[也需要配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，否则会话建立失败。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_980123068}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_980123070}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的发送时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[60]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname-te\] pce keepalive 60]{lang="EN-US"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- pce request-timeout**

宋体"}

[**[pce request-timeout ]{lang="EN-US"}***[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1377993319}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_198147488}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_198147486}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_198147491}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_198147492}

[*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504676}[：发送路径计算请求后等待应答的超时时间，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504679}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504678}[EPC]{lang="EN-US"}[方式下，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[发送计算请求后，如果在本命令指定的时间内没有收到计算应答，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[重新向该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[发送计算请求。如果仍然没有收到计算应答，则持续本过程直至收到为止。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504672}[BRPC]{lang="EN-US"}[方式下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[¡[  ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings"}[对于]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504675}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[设备，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[发送计算请求后，如果在本命令指定的时间内没有收到计算应答，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[认为请求失败，放弃计算请求。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[¡[  ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings"}[对于]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504674}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[设备，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[向它的下游]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[发送计算请求后，如果在本命令指定的时间内没有收到计算应答，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[直接向它的上游]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[应答本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的计算结果（如果是头节点]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[，则向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[反馈计算结果），不再等待下游]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的应答。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2140504668}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x184189540}[配置发送路径计算请求后等待应答的超时时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- pce tolerance**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[pce tolerance]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318692}[命令用来配置本地设备对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[对等体发送的消息的容忍度。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo pce tolerance]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318695}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318694}

[**[pce tolerance ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **min-keepalive**]{lang="EN-US"}*[ value \| ]{lang="EN-US"}***[max-unknown-messages]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*[ }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318688}

[**[undo pce tolerance ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **min-keepalive** \| ]{lang="EN-US"}**[max-unknown-messages ]{lang="EN-US"}**[}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318691}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318690}

[[能接受的对等体发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1382318684}[消息的最小时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[秒；能接受的对等体每分钟发送未知类型消息的最大个数为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_193822105}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_193822111}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_193822109}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_193822116}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800220}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800217}

[**[min-keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800223}[：指定能接受的对等体发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的最小时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[表示能接受任意的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[发送时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[max-unknown-messages]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800224}[：指定能接受的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[对等体每分钟发送未知类型消息的最大个数，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[16384]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[表示不限制对等体每分钟发送未知类型消息的最大个数。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1767800227}

[[对等体之间建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_958496155}[会话时，如果对端配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[发送时间间隔小于本地配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[min-keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**[的值，则将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[对端]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息的发送时间间隔协商为本端配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[min-keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}**[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果本地设备一分钟内从对等体接收到的未知消息数目大于或等于本地配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[max-unknown-messages]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_958496153}[，则断开与对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCEP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_958496159}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_958496158}[配置能接受的对等体的最小]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Keepalive]{lang="EN-US"}[消息发送时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20]{lang="EN-US"}[秒，每分钟发送未知类型消息的最大个数为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1380156005}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-te\] pce tolerance min-keepalive 20]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-te\] pce tolerance max-unknown-messages 10]{lang="EN-US"}

[*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_971472286}[：清除指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。如果不指定本参数，则清除所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCC]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PCE]{lang="EN-US"}[的统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- snmp-agent trap enable te**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[snmp-agent]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trap** **enable te**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1914460607}[命令用来开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[模块的告警功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **snmp-agent** **trap** **enable te**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1621306705}[命令用来关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[模块的告警功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1842882558}

[**[snmp-agent]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trap** **enable** **te**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1914132927}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **snmp-agent** **trap** **enable** **te**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1799201849}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x404875512}[模块的告警功能处于开启状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x725337876}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1914263999}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x579879962}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1914198463}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_386935778}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_486439726}

[[开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1913870783}[模块的告警功能后，当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道状态发生变化时会产生]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RFC 3812]{lang="EN-US"}[中规定的告警信息。生成的告警信息将发送到设备的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SNMP]{lang="EN-US"}[模块，通过设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SNMP]{lang="EN-US"}[中告警信息的发送参数，来决定告警信息输出的相关属性。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[有关告警信息的详细介绍，请参见"网络管理和监控配置指导"中的"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SNMP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x137254682}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1913805247}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1934827920}[开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}[模块的告警功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1449912857}

[\[Sysname\] snmp-agent trap enable te]{lang="EN-US"}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- te-subtlv**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[te-subtlv]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x896210659}[命令用来配置携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[参数的各种子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo te-subtlv]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1470506366}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669283461}

[**[te-subtlv]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **bw-constraint** *value* \| **unreserved-subpool-bw** *value* } \*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_803933850}

[**[undo te-subtlv]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **bw-constraint** \| **unreserved-subpool-bw** } \*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669348997}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1257709769}

[[带宽约束]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bw-constraint]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_74536179}[的子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[252]{lang="EN-US"}[；子池未预订带宽]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unreserved-subpool-bw]{lang="EN-US"}**[的子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[251]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669152389}

[[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1751327606}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669217925}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_461513048}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669021317}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1330502937}

[**[bw-constraint]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669086853}[：指定带宽约束的子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[23]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[254]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[unreserved-subpool-bw]{lang="EN-US"}***[ value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x182872236}[：指定子池未预订带宽的子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[23]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[254]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_668890245}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Prestandard]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1051357538}[模式下，携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[参数的子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值未形成标准，不同厂商可能使用不同的类型值。为了实现不同厂商设备的互通，需要通过本命令手工配置这些子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_668955781}[模式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Prestandard]{lang="EN-US"}[时，本命令生效；模式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IETF]{lang="EN-US"}[时，本命令不生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_604747513}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1472083969}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[进程]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DS-TE]{lang="EN-US"}[参数的各种子]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[带宽约束]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[bw-constraint]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669807749}[的子]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[200]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[子池未预订带宽]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[unreserved-subpool-bw]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1967173577}[的子]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[TLV]{lang="EN-US"}[类型值为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[202]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669873285}

[\[Sysname\] isis 1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-isis-1\] te-subtlv bw-constraint 200 unreserved-]{lang="EN-US"}[subpool-]{lang="EN-US"}[bw 202]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_360861927}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display isis mpls te configured-sub-tlvs]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669283462}
:::

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- timers removal unused**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[timers removal unused]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669348998}[命令用来配置空闲]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道的自动清除时间。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo timers removal unused]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1257709768}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669152390}

[**[timers removal unused ]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_204987537}*[seconds]{lang="FR"}*

[**[undo timers removal unused]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669217926}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_461513045}

[[空闲]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669021318}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道的自动清除时间]{style="font-family:宋体"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3600]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1330502940}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669086854}[自动隧道备份视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x182872229}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1276075066}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_668890246}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1051357535}

[*[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_668955782}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[空闲]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道的自动清除时间，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[300]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[604800]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[表示禁止清除空闲的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_604747510}

[[一条自动创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669807750}[隧道可以与多条主隧道绑定。当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道没有与任何主隧道绑定时，该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道称为空闲]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。自动创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的空闲时间到达本命令配置的值时，自动清除该隧道，以释放该隧道占用的带宽资源和接口编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令配置的值如果过大，则会导致]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_10858434}[隧道长时间占用带宽资源和接口编号；本命令配置的值如果过小，则可能会导致]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道频繁建立和拆除。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669873286}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_360861928}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[空闲]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="FR"}[隧道的自动清除时间]{style="font-family:宋体"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[分钟。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669283459}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te\] auto-tunnel backup]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te-auto-bk\] timers removal unused 60000]{lang="FR"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669348995}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="FR" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[tunnel-number]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1257709771}

**MPLS TE \-- MPLS TE配置命令 \-- tunnel-number**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[tunnel-number]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1751327604}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[自动创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的接口编号范围]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo tunnel-number]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669217923}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_461513042}

[**[tunnel-number min]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669021315}*[ min-number]{lang="FR"}***[ max ]{lang="FR"}***[max-number]{lang="FR"}*

[**[undo tunnel-number]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1330502935}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_2096102829}

[[未指定自动创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669086851}[隧道的接口编号范围，不能自动创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x182872234}

[[MPLS TE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_668890243}[自动隧道备份视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1051357532}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_668955779}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_649377521}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669807747}

[**[min]{lang="FR"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1967173571}*[ ]{lang="FR"}[min-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[：指定自动创建]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的接口编号的最小值。本参数的取值范围与设备型号有关，请以设备实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[max ]{lang="FR"}***[max-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669873283}[：指定自动创建]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的接口编号的最大值。本参数的取值范围与设备型号有关，请以设备实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_360861933}

[[通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669283460}[命令全局开启自动隧道备份功能后，还需执行本命令，才能自动建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道；否则，无法自动建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。自动建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道时，设备按照由小到大的顺序从本命令指定的范围内依次为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道选择接口编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_803933851}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[min-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669348996}[必须小于等于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[max-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置的接口编号范围不能大于]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_1257709770}[1000]{lang="EN-US"}[，否则命令无法成功执行。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[重复执行本命令，新的配置将覆盖原有配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669152388}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[重复执行本命令修改接口编号范围时，修改后的编号范围需要包含已经自动创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x1751327607}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的接口编号，否则命令无法成功执行。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[即：如果在修改编号范围前已经自动创建了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道，则]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[min-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[不能大于这些]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道中的接口编号的最小值；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[max-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[不能小于这些]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道中的接口编号的最大值。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[interface tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_x2031474917}[命令手工创建隧道接口时，指定的隧道接口编号可以在本命令配置的范围内，该编号不会再用于自动创建的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道；通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo interface tunnel]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令删除该隧道接口后，该接口编号仍然可以用于自动创建的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669217924}

[[\# ]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_461513047}[配置自动创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Bypass]{lang="EN-US"}[隧道的接口编号范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[800]{lang="EN-US"}[到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[900]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="FR"}]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669021316}

[\[Sysname\] mpls te]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te\] auto-tunnel backup]{lang="FR"}

[\[Sysname-te-auto-bk\] tunnel-number min 800 max 900]{lang="FR"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[auto-tunnel backup]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1870_x2080_669086852}
:::
